We introduce a curve-guided Gaussian representation that models strokes as sequences of anisotropic primitives along continuous trajectories, whose smooth kernels capture the soft boundaries of sand strokes and enable coherent stroke formation. We further adopt a subtractive compositing scheme to model light attenuation during sand accumulation. We incorporate a semantic-guided planning module for scene decomposition and drawing order inference. Our framework jointly optimizes stroke geometry and appearance and can be integrated with a physics-based simulator for interactive sand dynamics and editing. Experiments show that our method produces temporally coherent and visually realistic results, achieving improved reconstruction quality and perceptual fidelity compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [184] [Line Segment Clipping using Quadrilateral Concavity and Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27701)
*Bimal Kumar Ray*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于四边形凹凸性判断的线段裁剪算法，通过将裁剪边界和线段均视为线段，避免计算虚假交点，相比传统算法性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线段裁剪算法（如Cohen-Sutherland、Liang-Barsky等）在计算交点时会产生虚假交点，导致不必要的除法运算，增加计算开销。本文旨在通过避免虚假交点的计算来提高裁剪效率。

Method: 将裁剪边界和被裁剪线段均视为线段，以裁剪边界端点与线段端点构成四边形，通过判断四边形的凹凸性来决定线段是否真正与裁剪边界相交：若四边形为凹形则拒绝线段，若为凸形则计算交点。采用"先测试后求交"策略替代传统的"先求交后测试"策略。

Result: 实验表明，该算法在基于执行时间的度量下，性能优于Nicholl-Lee-Nicholl、Liang-Barsky、Cohen-Sutherland和Skala等经典算法。

Conclusion: 通过避免计算虚假交点，减少了除法运算次数，只需单一例程即可处理任意位置的线段，提升了裁剪效率。

Abstract: This paper proposes an algorithm for clipping line segment against an axis-aligned rectangular window. The conventional algorithms for line segment clipping treat the clipping boundary and/or the line segment to be clipped as line. The present algorithm treats the clipping boundary and the line segment to be clipped as line segment and using this strategy, it succeeds to avoid computation of false intersection points. A quadrilateral is constructed using the end points of a clipping boundary segment and the end points of the line segment to be clipped as its vertices. The concavity and convexity of the quadrilateral dictates whether a line segment actually intersects the clipping boundary. If the quadrilateral is found to be concave then the line segment is rejected, otherwise the point of intersection of the line segment with the clipping boundary is computed. Since a 'test & intersect' approach is used instead of a 'intersect & test', hence the proposed algorithm does not compute false intersection point thereby reducing the number of divisions required to obtain a clipped line segment. Only one routine can process line segments at any position. Improved performance is observed with respect to the Nicholl-Lee-Nicholl, Liang-Barsky, Cohen-Sutherland and Skala's algorithm through experiments with random line segments using a metric based on execution time.

</details>


### [185] [D-Rex : Diffusion Rendering for Relightable Expressive Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27871)
*Timo Teufel,Xilong Zhou,Umar Iqbal,Jan Kautz,Marc Habermann,Vladislav Golyanik,Christian Theobalt*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: D-Rex提出了一种将重光照与人体数字化身建模解耦的框架，通过将重光照视为图像空间的后处理过程，利用预训练的视频扩散模型和LoRA微调，在白光照明的化身系统上实现可重光照、可表达、可动画的全身逼真化身，无需修改现有化身管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可重光照全身化身方法依赖显式3D本征分解和分析反射模型，需要精确几何配准和精心优化，且难以同时实现强面部动画与重光照，限制了在远程临场、游戏和虚拟制作中的应用。

Method: 将重光照从化身建模中完全解耦，视为图像空间后处理：利用预训练视频扩散重光照模型（通过LoRA在光舞台采集的配对数据上微调），对独立训练的白色光照化身框架生成的平坦光照类反照率渲染图进行翻译，生成目标HDR光照下的结果。

Result: D-Rex实现了视角一致和时间一致的重光照，同时忠实保留表达性动作和细粒度面部细节，在物理基础的可重光照化身基线上表现更优。

Conclusion: 重光照与化身建模解耦的策略是有效的，可直接应用于任何白色光照化身系统，无需修改即可支持重光照功能，突破了现有方法在表达性和可重光照性之间的权衡限制。

Abstract: We present D-Rex, a person-specific framework for photorealistic, relightable, expressive, and animatable full-body human avatars with free-viewpoint rendering. Existing methods for relightable full-body avatars rely on explicit 3D intrinsic decomposition with analytic reflectance models, which require accurate geometry registration and careful optimization to capture realistic light transport effects. This tight coupling of relighting with avatar modeling has hindered expressiveness: to our knowledge, no existing method demonstrates strong facial animation alongside relighting, limiting applicability in telepresence, gaming, and virtual production. We propose to decouple relighting entirely from avatar modeling by treating it as an image-space post-process: a learned translation from flat-lit, albedo-like renderings to a target HDR illumination. To this end, we leverage the strong generative prior of a pre-trained video diffusion relighting model, fine-tuned via LoRA on paired flat-lit and relit frames captured in a light stage. The flat-lit driving frames are produced by an independent expressive full-body avatar framework trained under white-light conditions, requiring no modification to support relighting, making D-Rex directly applicable to any white-light avatar system. We demonstrate that D-Rex enables view- and temporally consistent relighting while faithfully preserving expressive motion and fine-grained facial detail, outperforming physically-based relightable avatar baselines. Project page is https://vcai.mpi-inf.mpg.de/projects/DRex/

</details>
